134 /// \brief Contains a OCPP CallError message
135 struct CallError {
136 MessageId uniqueId;
137 std::string errorCode;
138 std::string errorDescription;
139 json errorDetails;
140
141 /// \brief Creates a new CallError message object
142 CallError();
143
144 /// \brief Creates a new CallResult message object with the given \p uniqueID \p errorCode \p errorDescription and
145 /// \p errorDetails
146 CallError(const MessageId& uniqueId, const std::string& errorCode, const std::string& errorDescription,
147 const json& errorDetails);
148 };
149
